Generated UI design report

Advisory evaluation of an existing preview. Not a release gate or proof of backend behavior.

2026-09-09T13:22:44.628Z · Download full evidence JSON

Spend import review

Synthetic review scenario grounded in implemented Vendor transaction-import and derived-fact substrate; the proposed reconciliation decision contract is a planned extension.

Case evidence and review questions
  • arrusted: apps/vendor/schema/vendor.cue — implemented
  • ag2: backend/src/services/vendor/derivedFacts.ts — implemented
  • arrusted: docs/plans/2026-07-23-vendor-spend-reconciliation-review-contract.md — planned

Review questions

  • Can a reviewer verify field mappings and preview the changes before saving?
  • Can a reviewer see uncertain matches, source provenance, and the bounded correction without overwriting source evidence?

Intended outcomes, not asserted results

  • A user can save an import after checking its mappings and change preview.
  • The save outcome separates imported transactions from uncertain matches that remain in review.

Arrusted adherence: 96/100 · partial

Evidence coverage: 2.76%. Evaluator 3. This measures inspected evidence, not all rendered UI.

component 30 0 7 100% (30/30) 37 / 0
api 47 7 20 87.04% (47/54) 74 / 0
styling 28 0 3918 100% (28/28) 28 / 3918

Equal average of available dimension percentages; each is conforming / assessed. Shared-library observations are excluded. Unknown provenance is unassessed. Static JSX is not proof of rendering. Coverage describes inspected evidence, not the entire app.

  • Conservative matched-style evidence, not a full CSS cascade or React component provenance proof.
  • Conflicting declarations, unknown variables and shorthand-only properties remain unassessed. Sampling is capped at 120 eligible elements and diversified by rendered region.
  • A shared bundle is never treated as positive generated adherence. Anonymous rules require a unique exact generated selector/property/value and complete matched-rule declaration set; conditional or ambiguous source rules remain unknown.
  • Intrinsic tag/class matches are reviewer-facing generated-origin candidates only. Shared components can assemble identical classes dynamically, so they remain unknown and unassessed without direct stylesheet provenance.
  • No generated CSS source files were supplied; compiled stylesheet source maps cannot establish generated declaration provenance.

review actions do not rewrite it.",tone:"info"},{type:"field",id:"vendor",label:"Vendor (raw)",value:selected.vendorRaw},{type:"field",id:"amount",label:"Amount",value:`${selected.currency} ${selected.amount.toFixed(2)}`},{type:"field",id:"date",label:"Transaction date",value:selected.transactionDate},{type:"field",id:"ref",label:"Source reference",value:selected.sourceRef}]},{id:"why",title:"Why this row is exceptional",items:[{type:"note",id:"issue",title:selected.issue,body:"A finance operator must choose or leave this unresolved.",tone:"warning"}]},{id:"action",title:"Bounded next action",items:[{type:"note",id:"options",body:"Selections and outcomes are in-memory simulation only.",tone:"neutral"}]}]} renderItem={item=>item.id==="options"?<div className="grid gap-[var(--space-3)]">{selected.candidates.map(c=><DecisionOptionCard key={c.id} name={`choice-${selected.id}`} value={c.id} title={c.title} description={c.description} selected={choice===c.id} density="compact" tag={choice===c.id?<Tag tone="info" size="md">Selected</Tag>:undefined} onSelect={()=>{setChoices(x=>({...x,[selected.id]:c.id}));setOutcomes(x=>{const n={...x};delete n[selected.id];return n})}}/>)}{outcome?<Typography variant="body-strong">{outcome==="resolved"?"Simulated decision recorded.":"Left unresolved for follow-up."}</Typography>:null}</div>:undefined} actions={[{id:"preview",label:"Back to import summary",intent:"secondary"},{id:"defer",label:"Leave unresolved",intent:"secondary"},{id:"apply",label:"Apply simulated decision",intent:"primary-strong",disabled:!choice||Boolean(outcome)}]} onAction={id=>{if(id==="preview")setStep("preview");if(id==="defer")setOutcomes(x=>({...x,[selected.id]:"deferred"}));if(id==="apply"&&choice)setOutcomes(x=>({...x,[selected.id]:"resolved"}))}}/>}/>:null}

review actions do not rewrite it.",tone:"info"},{type:"field",id:"vendor",label:"Vendor (raw)",value:selected.vendorRaw},{type:"field",id:"amount",label:"Amount",value:`${selected.currency} ${selected.amount.toFixed(2)}`},{type:"field",id:"date",label:"Transaction date",value:selected.transactionDate},{type:"field",id:"ref",label:"Source reference",value:selected.sourceRef}]},{id:"why",title:"Why this row is exceptional",items:[{type:"note",id:"issue",title:selected.issue,body:"A finance operator must choose or leave this unresolved.",tone:"warning"}]},{id:"action",title:"Bounded next action",items:[{type:"note",id:"options",body:"Selections and outcomes are in-memory simulation only.",tone:"neutral"}]}]} renderItem={item=>item.id==="options"?<div className="grid gap-[var(--space-3)]">{selected.candidates.map(c=><DecisionOptionCard key={c.id} name={`choice-${selected.id}`} value={c.id} title={c.title} description={c.description} selected={choice===c.id} density="compact" tag={choice===c.id?<Tag tone="info" size="md">Selected</Tag>:undefined} onSelect={()=>{setChoices(x=>({...x,[selected.id]:c.id}));setOutcomes(x=>{const n={...x};delete n[selected.id];return n})}}/>)}{outcome?<Typography variant="body-strong">{outcome==="resolved"?"Simulated decision recorded.":"Left unresolved for follow-up."}</Typography>:null}</div>:undefined} actions={[{id:"preview",label:"Back to import summary",intent:"secondary"},{id:"defer",label:"Leave unresolved",intent:"secondary"},{id:"apply",label:"Apply simulated decision",intent:"primary-strong",disabled:!choice||Boolean(outcome)}]} onAction={id=>{if(id==="preview")setStep("preview");if(id==="defer")setOutcomes(x=>({...x,[selected.id]:"deferred"}));if(id==="apply"&&choice)setOutcomes(x=>({...x,[selected.id]:"resolved"}))}}/>}/>:null}
45:   </section></div><Typography variant="caption" tone="muted">Synthetic fixture · All mapping, import, and review changes are in-memory simulations. Automated reconciliation is not represented as shipped behavior.</Typography>

AI design review

Subjective design score: 75/100

One model assessment; not an objective rating. Token adherence is reported separately below.

Dimension Rating Reason
hierarchy 3/4 The three-step rail, page title, current-state treatments, preview counts, and exception-detail sections establish clear priorities. The source evidence, exception reason, and bounded next action are especially easy to scan, though narrow-window summary pills wrap awkwardly and some small status labels lose emphasis.
layout 3/4 Mapping rows align source samples directly with destination fields, the preview table is compact, and the wide exception view keeps the queue beside the selected record. Spacing is consistent, but the 1024px preview clips the import-result column and the review action area sits far down a long document.
typography 3/4 Titles, section headings, field labels, and values use a consistent, readable hierarchy. Secondary metadata and several 10–12px status or caption labels are comparatively faint and small; automated evidence also flags recurring advisory contrast concerns for these labels.
responsive 3/4 The composition works at 1024, 1440, and 1920px: mapping controls contract, the wide list-detail review becomes a focused detail view with a Back control, and primary actions remain usable. The 1024px preview relies on horizontal scrolling and visibly truncates the rightmost result content, preventing an excellent score.
productClarity 3/4 Reviewers can verify mappings, inspect representative changes before saving, see the 38-ready/4-review split, and receive an explicit saved outcome. Exception detail keeps immutable raw values, provenance, the ambiguity explanation, two bounded vendor choices, and a defer option together. However, Apply appears available without a visible selection, and the tested chosen-match path produced no visible confirmation.

Strengths

  • The workflow directly answers the brief with mapping, preview, save outcome, and exception-review stages.
  • Sample source values remain beside each mapping, making field verification concrete rather than abstract.
  • The preview clearly distinguishes 38 ready rows from 4 rows needing review and offers a route back to mapping before saving.
  • The saved state explicitly states that 38 rows were imported and 4 remain in human review rather than implying full reconciliation.
  • Exception detail clearly labels the original source row as preserved evidence and shows batch, source, raw vendor, amount, date, and source reference.
  • The ambiguity is explicit: two candidate identities are described with differentiating evidence, while Leave unresolved provides a bounded alternative.
  • Wide and constrained desktop layouts adapt thoughtfully; the constrained review preserves a focused detail experience with a clear route back to exception rows.

Recommended improvements

medium · desktop-window-1

At the 1024px desktop window, the preview table’s rightmost Import result column is visibly cut off and requires horizontal scrolling. This hides the most decision-relevant result for several rows during pre-save review.

Improve: Use the compact table density and rebalance column widths, or move lower-priority source metadata into a secondary line so Import result remains visible. If horizontal scrolling is retained, provide a clear, keyboard-accessible scroll affordance.

Screenshot region: x 349, y 221, width 630, height 240

low · desktop-window-1

The 38 ready and 4 need review summaries wrap into tall, nearly circular pills at this width, making the counts less immediately scannable than in the wider captures.

Improve: Place the status summary in a wrapping row beneath the preview heading or allocate it a wider column, preserving the existing supported pill variants and palette.

Screenshot region: x 820, y 141, width 159, height 54

high · desktop-3

Neither vendor option has a visible selected state, yet Apply simulated decision appears available. The supplied interaction for recording a chosen match also failed to produce the expected confirmation, leaving the reviewer uncertain whether a correction was recorded.

Improve: Keep Apply disabled until one candidate is visibly selected. After applying, show persistent inline confirmation, update the selected row’s status and remaining count, and retain the original source section unchanged.

Screenshot region: x 808, y 751, width 584, height 256

medium · desktop-4

After the row is marked Deferred and an inline follow-up message appears, Leave unresolved and Apply simulated decision remain presented with the same prominence. This makes it unclear whether the defer action can be repeated or whether a new candidate choice is required to change the decision.

Improve: Treat Deferred as a recorded decision state: disable or relabel the repeated defer action, and only enable Apply after a candidate is selected to replace that decision.

Screenshot region: x 807, y 778, width 585, height 260

low · desktop-3

Exception-row provenance is useful, but dates, references, and Needs review badges are rendered in very small secondary text. The automated evidence repeatedly flags these captions and badges as difficult to read against their surfaces.

Improve: Use the supported medium Tag size or a primary-emphasis label treatment for decision-critical status text, and reduce reliance on tiny captions without changing the authoritative palette.

Screenshot region: x 329, y 206, width 438, height 284
